				WHERE A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 3403	 
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD,  ISNULL((SELECT SUM(C.SALDO) 
																			 FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS  C  WITH(NOLOCK)
																			 WHERE C.DIASATRASOALERTA IN ('V013','V014','V015','V016')
																			 AND C.IDENTIDAD= @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO), 0) 
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE  A.CUENTACONTABLE = 'SD(20002)' AND A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 0
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, ISNULL((SELECT C.FLUCTUACION 
																			 FROM HIST_TBP C  WITH(NOLOCK)
																			 WHERE C.FECHA BETWEEN @PERIODO AND DATEADD(DAY, 32, @PERIODO)), 0)
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE = 'QC(40000)' AND A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 0
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD,  @TBP 
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE = 'SD(90017)' AND A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 0
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, ISNULL((SELECT C.FLUCTUACION 
																			 FROM HIST_LIBOR C  WITH(NOLOCK)
																			 WHERE C.FECHA BETWEEN @PERIODO AND DATEADD(DAY, 32, @PERIODO)), 0) 
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE = 'QC(45000)' AND A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 0
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD,  @LIBOR
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE = 'SD(90016)' AND A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 0
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, ISNULL((SELECT SUM(C.SALDOFINAL)
																			 FROM XML_CONTABLE_ESTADO_HIS C  WITH(NOLOCK)
																			 WHERE C.IDENCABEZADO_ID = (SELECT MAX(ID) 
																									  FROM XML_ENCABEZADO_HIS WITH(NOLOCK)
																									  WHERE IDENTIDAD_ID = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO
																									  AND IDARCHIVO_ID = 201
																									  AND IDESTADO_ID = 3)
																									 AND SUBSTRING(C.CUENTA, 1, 1) = '1'
																		AND SUBSTRING(C.CUENTA, 3, 1) != '9'
																		AND SUBSTRING(C.CUENTA, 6, 3) = '200'), 0)
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE = 'QC(10000)' AND A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 0
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, ISNULL((SELECT SUM(C.SALDOFINAL)
																			 FROM XML_CONTABLE_ESTADO_HIS C  WITH(NOLOCK)
																			 WHERE C.IDENCABEZADO_ID = (SELECT MAX(ID) 
																										  FROM XML_ENCABEZADO_HIS WITH(NOLOCK)
																										  WHERE IDENTIDAD_ID = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO
																										  AND IDARCHIVO_ID = 201
																										  AND IDESTADO_ID = 3)
																			AND SUBSTRING(C.CUENTA, 1, 1) = '2'
																			AND SUBSTRING(C.CUENTA, 6, 3) = '200'), 0) 
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE = 'QC(20000)' AND A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 0
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, ISNULL((SELECT C.FLUCTUACION 
																			 FROM HIST_TIPOCAMBIO C  WITH(NOLOCK)
																			 WHERE C.FECHA BETWEEN @PERIODO AND DATEADD(DAY, 32, @PERIODO)), 0) 
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE = 'QC(30000)' AND A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 0
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @TC 
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE = 'SD(90011)' AND A.IDARCHIVO_ID = 0
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, ISNULL((SELECT C.FLUCTUACION1M 
																			 FROM SALIDA_VARIACIONFLUCTUACION C  WITH(NOLOCK)
																			 WHERE C.CUENTA = A.CUENTACOMPLETA
																			 AND C.IDENTIDAD= @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO), 0)
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE IN ('QC(70000)', 'QC(70100)', 'QC(70200)', 'QC(90000)', 'QC(90100)', 'QC(90200)')
				UNION
				SELECT A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, ISNULL((SELECT C.FLUCTUACION3M 
																			 FROM SALIDA_VARIACIONFLUCTUACION C  WITH(NOLOCK)
																			 WHERE C.CUENTA = A.CUENTACOMPLETA
																			 AND C.IDENTIDAD= @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO), 0) 
				FROM  RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE A.CUENTACONTABLE IN ('QC(80000)', 'QC(80100)', 'QC(80200)', 'QC(100000)', 'QC(100100)', 'QC(100200)')
				UNION
				SELECT  A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, (SELECT  ISNULL(ABS(SUM(D.MONTO)), 0)
																  FROM (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(C.SALDOFINAL), 0) AS MONTO
																		 FROM SALIDA_BALANCE_COMPROBACION C WITH(NOLOCK)
																		 WHERE C.CUENTA = A.CUENTACOMPLETA
																		 AND C.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO <= @PERIODO AND PERIODO >= DATEADD(MONTH, -11, @PERIODO)) D)
			  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL] A
			  WHERE CUENTACONTABLE LIKE 'AC%'
			  UNION
			  SELECT  A.ID, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, (SELECT  ISNULL((SUM(D.MONTO)), 0)
																	  FROM (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(C.SALDOFINAL), 0) AS MONTO
																			 FROM SALIDA_BALANCE_COMPROBACION C WITH(NOLOCK)
																			 WHERE C.CUENTA = A.CUENTACOMPLETA
																			 AND C.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO <= @PERIODO AND PERIODO >= DATEADD(MONTH, -2, @PERIODO)) D)
			  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[RPT_ESTRUCTURA_CAMEL] A
			  WHERE CUENTACONTABLE LIKE 'TC%'
			  END 

			  /*Si no existe el dato 3221 en indicadores financieros se calcula manualmente*/
			  SELECT @SD20005 = ISNULL(MONTO, 0) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 6
			  IF @SD20005 = 0 BEGIN
				
					UPDATE #RESUMEN
					SET MONTO =  ISNULL((SELECT C.MONTOVALOR FROM XML_INDICADORES_FINANCIEROS_HIS C  WITH(NOLOCK)
										 WHERE C.CUENTACATALOGO = 3111 AND C.IDENCABEZADO_ID IN (SELECT ID 
																								 FROM XML_ENCABEZADO_HIS WITH(NOLOCK)
																								 WHERE IDENTIDAD_ID = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO
																								 AND IDARCHIVO_ID = 3403 AND IDESTADO_ID = 3)), 0)
							+ ISNULL((SELECT SUM(C.SALDOFINAL) 
								   FROM SALIDA_BALANCE_COMPROBACION C  WITH(NOLOCK)
								   WHERE C.CUENTA IN ('12900000','13900000', '25200000') AND IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO), 0)
					WHERE ID IN (6, 32)

			  END

			END

			/*CAPITAL PRIMARIO*/
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM SALIDA_SUFICIENCIA WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND
																PERIODO = @PERIODO AND IDRPT_SUFICIENCIA = 3)
		WHERE ID IN (18, 359)

		/*CAPITAL SECUNDARIO*/
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM SALIDA_SUFICIENCIA WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND
																PERIODO = @PERIODO AND IDRPT_SUFICIENCIA = 18)
		WHERE ID IN (19, 360)

		/*CAPITAL SECUNDARIO*/
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM SALIDA_SUFICIENCIA WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND
																PERIODO = @PERIODO AND IDRPT_SUFICIENCIA = 56)
		WHERE ID IN (20, 361)

		/*PERDIDAS ESPERADAS EN ACTIVOS - ESTIMACIONES*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM SALIDA_SUFICIENCIA WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND
																PERIODO = @PERIODO AND IDRPT_SUFICIENCIA = 2)
		WHERE ID IN (17, 358)

		/*CAPITAL BASE*/
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(CASE ID WHEN 359 THEN MONTO WHEN 360 THEN MONTO ELSE -MONTO END) 
					FROM #RESUMEN 
					WHERE ID IN (359, 360, 361))
		WHERE ID = 358

		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = ABS((SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 354) - (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 355))
		WHERE ID = 353

		/*NUMERADOR DE RIESGO CAMBIARIO*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 353) *
						(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 356) /
						(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 357)
			WHERE ID = 352
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 352
		END CATCH 

		/*RIESGO CAMBIARIO*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * 100 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 352) /
						(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 358) 
			WHERE ID = 351
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 351
		END CATCH 

		/*DENOMINADOR DE RIESGO POR TASAS DE INTERES EN M.E.*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (1 + @LIBOR / 100) /
						 (ISNULL((SELECT C.FLUCTUACION FROM HIST_LIBOR C  WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE C.FECHA BETWEEN @PERIODO AND DATEADD(DAY, 32, @PERIODO)), 0))
			WHERE ID = 348
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 348
		END CATCH 

			/*DENOMINADOR DE RIESGO POR TASAS DE INTERES EN M.E.*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = ABS((SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 345) - (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 347) *
						((SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 346)/(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 344)))
			WHERE ID = 343
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 343
		END CATCH 

		/*RIESGO POR TASAS DE INTERES DOLARES*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * 100 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 343) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 348)
			WHERE ID = 342
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 342
		END CATCH 

		/*DENOMINADOR DE RIESGO POR TASAS DE INTERES EN COLONES*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (1 + @TBP / 100) /
						(ISNULL((SELECT C.FLUCTUACION 
																		 FROM HIST_TBP C  WITH(NOLOCK)
																		 WHERE C.FECHA BETWEEN @PERIODO AND DATEADD(DAY, 32, @PERIODO)), 0))
			WHERE ID = 339
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 339
		END CATCH 

			/*DENOMINADOR DE RIESGO POR TASAS DE INTERES EN COLONES*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = ABS((SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 336) - (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 338) *
						((SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 337)/(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 335)))
			WHERE ID = 334
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 334
		END CATCH 

		/*RIESGO POR TASAS DE INTERES COLONES*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 334) / ((SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 339) / 100)		
			WHERE ID = 333
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 333
		END CATCH 

		/*OTRAS OBLIGACIONES CON EL PUBLICO A LA VISTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 315 AND 326)
		WHERE ID = 314

		/*CAPTACIONES A LA VISTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 308 AND 313)
		WHERE ID = 307

		/*VENCIMIENTO DE PASIVOS A TRES MESES*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * -1 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 303) *
					(1 - (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 304)) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 305) *
					(1 - (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 306)) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 307) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 314) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 327) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 328) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 329) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 330) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 331) 
		WHERE ID = 302
		
		/*RECUPERACION ACTIVOS A TRES MESES ME*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 297 AND 301)
		WHERE ID = 296

		/*CALCE DE PLAZOS A TRES MESES AJUSTADO POR LA VOLATILIDAD DE ME*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 296) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 302)
			WHERE ID = 295
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 295
		END CATCH 

		/*OTRAS OBLIGACIONES CON EL PUBLICO A LA VISTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 278 AND 289)
		WHERE ID = 277

		/*CAPTACIONES A LA VISTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 271 AND 276)
		WHERE ID = 270

		/*VENCIMIENTO DE PASIVOS A TRES MESES*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * -1 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 266) *
					(1 - (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 267)) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 268) *
					(1 - (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 269)) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 270) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 277) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 290) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 291) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 292) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 293) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 294) 
		WHERE ID = 265
	
		/*RECUPERACION ACTIVOS A TRES MESES MN*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 260 AND 264)
		WHERE ID = 259

			/*CALCE DE PLAZOS A TRES MESES AJUSTADO POR LA VOLATILIDAD DE ME*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 259) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 265)
			WHERE ID = 258
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 258
		END CATCH 

		/*OTRAS OBLICACIONES CON EL PUBLICO A LA VISTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 243 AND 254)
		WHERE ID = 242

		/*OTRAS OBLICACIONES CON EL PUBLICO A LA VISTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 236 AND 241)
		WHERE ID = 235
	
		/*VENCIMIENTO DE PASIVOS A UN MES ME*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 235) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 242) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 255) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 256) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 257) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 231) *
					(1 - (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 232)) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 233) *
					(1 - (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 234)) 
		WHERE ID = 230

		/*RECUPERACION ACTIVOS A UN MES ME*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 227 AND 229)
		WHERE ID = 226

		/*CALCE DE PLAZOS A TRES MESES AJUSTADO POR LA VOLATILIDAD DE ME*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 226) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 230)
			WHERE ID = 225
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 225
		END CATCH 

		/*OTRAS OBLIGACIONES CON PUBLICO A LA VISTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 210 AND 221)
		WHERE ID = 209

		/*CAPTACIONES A LA VISTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 203 AND 208)
		WHERE ID = 202

		/*VENCIMIENTO DE PASIVOS A UN MES MN*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = -(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 198) *
					(1-(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 199)) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 200) *
					(1-(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 201)) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 202) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 209) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 222) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 223) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 224) 		
		WHERE ID = 197
	
		/*RECUPERACION ACTIVOS A UN MES MN*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 194 AND 196)
		WHERE ID = 193

		/*CALCE DE PLAZOS A TRES MESES AJUSTADO POR LA VOLATILIDAD DE MN*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 193) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 197)
			WHERE ID = 192
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 192
		END CATCH 

		/*VENCIMIENTO DE PASIVOS A TRES MESES*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = -(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 175) *
					(1-(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 176)) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 177) *
					(1-(SELECT C.FLUCTUACION3M 
					 FROM SALIDA_VARIACIONFLUCTUACION C  WITH(NOLOCK)
					 WHERE C.CUENTA = '21103000' AND C.IDENTIDAD= @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO 
					)) + (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 181) +
					(SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 183 AND 186) +
					(SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 188 AND 190) 	
		WHERE ID = 174
	 	

		/*RECUPERACION ACTIVOS A TRES MESES*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 165 AND 173)
		WHERE ID = 164

		/*CALCE DE PLAZOS A TRES MESES AJUSTADO POR LA VOLATILIDAD*/	
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 164) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 174)
			WHERE ID = 163
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 163
		END CATCH 

		/*VENCIMIENTO DE PASIVOS A TRES MESES*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = -(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 151) *
					(1-(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 152)) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 153) *
					(1-(SELECT C.FLUCTUACION1M 
						FROM SALIDA_VARIACIONFLUCTUACION C  WITH(NOLOCK)
						WHERE C.CUENTA = '21103000'
						AND C.IDENTIDAD= @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO)) + 
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 157) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 159) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 160) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 162) 
		WHERE ID = 150

		/*RECUPERACON ACTIVOS A UN MES*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 145 AND 149)
		WHERE ID = 144

		/*CALCE DE PLAZOS A UN MES AJUSTADO POR LA VOLATILIDAD*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 144) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 150)
			WHERE ID = 143
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 143
		END CATCH 

		/*RELACION PASIVOS ME/PASIVO*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * 100 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 140) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 141)
			WHERE ID = 139
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 139
		END CATCH 


		/*PATRIMONIO CONTABLE PROMEDIO TRIMESTRAL*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO/3) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 132 AND 136)
					+ (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 137)
					- (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 138)
		WHERE ID = 131

		/*UTILIDAD ACUMULADA TRIMESTRAL*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 129) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 130)
		WHERE ID = 128

		/*UTILIDAD ACUMULADA TRIMESTRAL SOBRE EL PATRIMONIO PROMEDIO TRIMESTRAL*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * 100 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 128) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 131)
			WHERE ID = 127
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 127
		END CATCH 

		/*UTILIDAD OPERACIONAL BRUTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 122 AND 123) -
					(SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 124 AND 125)
		WHERE ID = 121
	
		/*GASTOS DE ADMINISTRACION*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 120)
		WHERE ID = 119

		/*GASTOS DE ADMINISTRACION/UTILIDAD OPERACIONAL BRUTA*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * 100 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 119) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 121)
			WHERE ID = 118
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 118
		END CATCH 

		/*A PLAZO*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 100) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 101) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 102) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 103) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 104) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 105) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 106) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 107) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 108) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 109) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 110) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 111) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 112) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 113) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 114) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 115) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 116) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 117) 
		WHERE ID = 99

		/*A LA VISTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 76 AND 98)  
		WHERE ID = 75

		/*PASIVO CON COSTO*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 75) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 99)
		WHERE ID = 74

		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 62) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 63) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 64) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 65) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 66) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 67) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 68) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 69) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 70) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 71) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 72) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 73) 
		WHERE ID = 61

		/*MANEJO*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 61) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 74)
			WHERE ID = 60
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 60
		END CATCH 

			/*PASIVO CON COSTO*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 57) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 58)
		WHERE ID = 56
	
		/*OPERACIONES CREDITICIAS SUJETAS A ESTIMACION*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 35 AND 55)  
		WHERE ID = 34

		/*CARTERA TOTAL DE CREDITOS*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 34) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 56)
		WHERE ID = 33

		/*CARTERA TOTAL DE CREDITOS*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 32)
		WHERE ID = 31

		/*PERDIDA ESPERADA CARTERA TOTAL / CARTERA TOTAL*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * 100 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 31) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 33)
			WHERE ID = 30
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 30
		END CATCH 

		/*CARTERA DIRECTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 26 AND 29)  
		WHERE ID = 25

		/*CARTERA DIRECTA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 24)  
		WHERE ID = 23

		/*MOROSIDAD MAYOR A 90 DIAS / CARTERA DIRECTA*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * 100 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 23) / (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 25)
			WHERE ID = 22
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 22
		END CATCH 

		/*CAPITAL BASE*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(MONTO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 18 AND 19) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 20)
		WHERE ID = 17

		/*ESTIMACIONES*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(ABS(MONTO)) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 11 AND 15) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 16)
		WHERE ID = 10

		/*PERDIDAS ESPERADAS EN ACTIVOS*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT SUM(ABS(MONTO)) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID BETWEEN 7 AND 9) +
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 6)
		WHERE ID = 5

		/*PERDIDAS ESPERADAS EN ACTIVOS - ESTIMACIONES*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 5) -
					(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 10)
		WHERE ID = 4

		/*COMPROMISO PATRIMONIAL*/
		BEGIN TRY			
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO * 100 FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 4) /
						(SELECT MONTO FROM #RESUMEN WHERE ID = 17)
			WHERE ID = 3
		END TRY  
		BEGIN CATCH  
			UPDATE #RESUMEN
			SET MONTO = 0
			WHERE ID = 3
		END CATCH 

		/*SUFICIENCIA*/	
		UPDATE #RESUMEN
		SET MONTO = (SELECT MONTO FROM SALIDA_SUFICIENCIA WHERE IDRPT_SUFICIENCIA = 1 AND PERIODO = @PERIODO AND IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD) 
		WHERE ID = 0

		IF @EXISTE > 0 BEGIN
			INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_CAMEL]
				   ([IDRPT_CAMEL]
				   ,[PERIODO]
				   ,[IDENTIDAD]
				   ,[MONTO])
			SELECT ID, PERIODO, ENTIDAD, MONTO FROM #RESUMEN 
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Cartera_Credito]    Script Date: 23/08/2020 12:32:17 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Cartera_Credito]
	@IDENTIDAD NVARCHAR(5),
	@PERIODO DATE
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	DECLARE @CANTIDAD AS INT
	DECLARE @EXISTE AS  INT	
    SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO

	IF @EXISTE = 0 BEGIN

		SELECT @CANTIDAD = COUNT(1)
		FROM FGA.DBO.XML_ENCABEZADO A WITH(NOLOCK), FGA.DBO.ARCHIVOESTADO B, FGA.DBO.TIPOXML C
		WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO
		AND A.IdEntidad_Id = @IDENTIDAD
		AND A.IDESTADO_ID = B.ID AND B.ID = 3
		AND C.ID = A.IDARCHIVO_ID AND C.ID IN ('302','304','201', '303')

		IF(@CANTIDAD = 4) BEGIN
									
			INSERT INTO FGA.DBO.SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO (IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, DEUDOR, OPERACION, CATEGORIARIESGO, DIASATRASOMATRIS, DIASATRASOALERTA, TIPOCARTERA, SALDO)
			SELECT J.IDENTIDAD_ID, J.PERIODO, J.IDDEUDOR, J.IDOPERACION, A.CODIGO AS CATEGORIA_RIESGO,
			CASE J.ESTADOOPERACIONCREDITICIA WHEN 5 THEN 'COBRO JUDICIAL' ELSE B.NOMBRE END ATRASO_MATRIS, 
			CASE J.ESTADOOPERACIONCREDITICIA WHEN 5 THEN 'V015' ELSE C.CODIGO END ATRASO_ALERTATEMPRANA, D.CODIGO AS TIPOCARTERA, J.SALDO
			FROM FGA.DBO.CATEGORIA_RIESGO A, FGA.DBO.DIASATRASO B, FGA.DBO.DIASATRASO C, FGA.DBO.TIPOCARTERA D ,(
			SELECT B.IDENTIDAD_ID, B.PERIODO, A.IDDEUDOR, A.IDOPERACION,
					(SELECT C.CATEGORIARIESGO
							FROM XML_CREDITO_DEUDOR C WITH(NOLOCK), XML_ENCABEZADO D WITH(NOLOCK)
							WHERE C.IDDEUDOR = A.IDDEUDOR
								AND D.ID = C.IDENCABEZADO_ID
								AND D.PERIODO = @PERIODO
								AND D.IdEntidad_Id = @IDENTIDAD
								AND D.IDENTIDAD_ID = B.IDENTIDAD_ID
								AND D.IDESTADO_ID = 3
								AND D.IDARCHIVO_ID = '302') AS CATEGORIARIESGO, 
							(SELECT ISNULL(MAX(C.DIASATRASO), 0) 
							FROM XML_CREDITO_CUOTA_ATRASADA C WITH(NOLOCK), XML_ENCABEZADO D, DIASATRASO E
							WHERE  C.IDOPERACION = A.IDOPERACION
								AND D.ID = C.IDENCABEZADO_ID
								AND D.PERIODO = @PERIODO 								
								AND D.IdEntidad_Id = @IDENTIDAD
								AND D.IDENTIDAD_ID = B.IDENTIDAD_ID
								AND D.IDESTADO_ID = 3
								AND D.IDARCHIVO_ID = '304'
								AND C.DIASATRASO BETWEEN E.DIAATRASOINICIAL AND E.DIAATRASOFINAL
								AND E.APLICAALERTA = 1) AS DIASATRASO,	
							A.ESTADOOPERACIONCREDITICIA,
							A.TIPOCARTERA,
							A.SALDOPRINCIPAL  AS SALDO
					FROM XML_CREDITO_OPERACION_DIRIND A WITH(NOLOCK), XML_ENCABEZADO B WITH(NOLOCK)
					WHERE A.IDENCABEZADO_ID = B.ID
						AND B.PERIODO = @PERIODO 
						AND B.IDENTIDAD_ID = @IDENTIDAD
						AND B.IDESTADO_ID = 3
						AND B.IDARCHIVO_ID = '303') J
				WHERE J.CATEGORIARIESGO = A.CATEGORIARIESGO
				AND (J.DIASATRASO BETWEEN B.DIAATRASOINICIAL AND B.DIAATRASOFINAL AND B.APLICAMATRIZ = 1 )
				AND (J.DIASATRASO BETWEEN C.DIAATRASOINICIAL AND C.DIAATRASOFINAL AND C.APLICAALERTA = 1 )
				AND J.TIPOCARTERA = D.TIPOCARTERA
				AND J.IdEntidad_Id = @IDENTIDAD
				AND J.Periodo = @PERIODO		
		END
	END
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Categoria_Cartera]    Script Date: 23/08/2020 12:32:17 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Categoria_Cartera]
	@IDENTIDAD NVARCHAR(5),
	@PERIODO DATE
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	
	DECLARE @EXISTE BIGINT
	CREATE TABLE #RESUMEN(CATEGORIA NVARCHAR(2), CUOTA DECIMAL(35,5), SALDO DECIMAL(35,5))	
	
	SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO
	IF @EXISTE = 0 BEGIN

		INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (CATEGORIA, CUOTA, SALDO)
		SELECT  D.CATEGORIARIESGO, SUM(F.CUOTACREDITO), SUM(A.SALDO)
		FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A, FGA.DBO.CATEGORIA_RIESGO D, FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA_HIS F
		WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND DIASATRASOALERTA = 'V009'  AND D.CODIGO = A.CATEGORIARIESGO AND F.IDOPERACION = A.OPERACION AND F.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD 
			  AND F.PERIODO = A.PERIODO AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 
							FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] B
							WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
								  AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 
											  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] C
											  WHERE C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -2, @PERIODO) AND C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION))
		GROUP BY D.CATEGORIARIESGO


	END ELSE BEGIN
		
		SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO =  DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO)
		IF @EXISTE = 0 BEGIN

			INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (CATEGORIA, CUOTA, SALDO)
			SELECT  D.CATEGORIARIESGO, SUM(F.CUOTACREDITO), SUM(A.SALDO)
			FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A, FGA.DBO.CATEGORIA_RIESGO D, FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA F
			WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND DIASATRASOALERTA = 'V009'  AND D.CODIGO = A.CATEGORIARIESGO AND F.IDOPERACION = A.OPERACION AND F.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
				   AND F.PERIODO = A.PERIODO AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 
								FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] B
								WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
									  AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 
												  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] C
												  WHERE C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -2, @PERIODO) AND C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION))
			GROUP BY D.CATEGORIARIESGO

		END ELSE BEGIN

			INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (CATEGORIA, CUOTA, SALDO)
			SELECT  D.CATEGORIARIESGO, SUM(F.CUOTACREDITO), SUM(A.SALDO)
			FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A, FGA.DBO.CATEGORIA_RIESGO D, FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA F
			WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND DIASATRASOALERTA = 'V009'  AND D.CODIGO = A.CATEGORIARIESGO AND F.IDOPERACION = A.OPERACION AND F.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
				    AND F.PERIODO = A.PERIODO AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 
								FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] B
								WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
									  AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 
												  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] C
												  WHERE C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -2, @PERIODO) AND C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION))
			GROUP BY D.CATEGORIARIESGO
		END
	END


	INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CATEGORIA]
           ([INDNUEVA]
           ,[IDENTIDAD]
           ,[PERIODO]
           ,[A1]
           ,[A2]
           ,[B1]
           ,[B2]
           ,[C1]
           ,[C2]
           ,[D]
           ,[E]
		   ,SALDO)
     VALUES
           (0
           ,@IDENTIDAD
           ,@PERIODO
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'A1'), 0)
		   ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'A2'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'B1'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'B2'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'C1'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'C2'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'D'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'E'), 0)
		   ,ISNULL((SELECT SUM(SALDO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'X'), 0))

	DELETE #RESUMEN

		
	SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO
	IF @EXISTE = 0 BEGIN

		INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (CATEGORIA, CUOTA, SALDO)
		SELECT  D.CATEGORIARIESGO, SUM(F.CUOTACREDITO), SUM(A.SALDO)
		FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A, FGA.DBO.CATEGORIA_RIESGO D, FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA_HIS F
		WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD  AND D.CODIGO = A.CATEGORIARIESGO AND F.IDOPERACION = A.OPERACION AND F.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
				  AND F.PERIODO = A.PERIODO AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 
		 				FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] B
						WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
			   					AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
							  					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] C
												WHERE C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -2, @PERIODO) AND C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION))
		GROUP BY D.CATEGORIARIESGO

		
		INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (CATEGORIA, CUOTA, SALDO)
		SELECT  'X', 0, SUM(A.SALDO)
		FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A
		WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
		 				FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] B
						WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION)

	END ELSE BEGIN
		
		SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO =  DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO)
		IF @EXISTE = 0 BEGIN

			INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (CATEGORIA, CUOTA, SALDO)
			SELECT  D.CATEGORIARIESGO, SUM(F.CUOTACREDITO), SUM(A.SALDO)
			FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A, FGA.DBO.CATEGORIA_RIESGO D, FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA F
			WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD  AND D.CODIGO = A.CATEGORIARIESGO AND F.IDOPERACION = A.OPERACION AND F.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
				  AND F.PERIODO = A.PERIODO AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 
		 					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] B
							WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
			   					  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
							  					  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] C
												  WHERE C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -2, @PERIODO) AND C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION))
			GROUP BY D.CATEGORIARIESGO

					
			INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (CATEGORIA, CUOTA, SALDO)
			SELECT  'X', 0, SUM(A.SALDO)
			FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A
			WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
		 					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] B
							WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION)

		END ELSE BEGIN

			INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (CATEGORIA, CUOTA, SALDO)
			SELECT  D.CATEGORIARIESGO, SUM(F.CUOTACREDITO), SUM(A.SALDO)
			FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A, FGA.DBO.CATEGORIA_RIESGO D, FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA F
			WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD  AND D.CODIGO = A.CATEGORIARIESGO AND F.IDOPERACION = A.OPERACION AND F.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
				   AND F.PERIODO = A.PERIODO AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 
		 					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] B
							WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
			   					  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
							  					  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] C
												  WHERE C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -2, @PERIODO) AND C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION))
			GROUP BY D.CATEGORIARIESGO

			INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (CATEGORIA, CUOTA, SALDO)
			SELECT  'X', 0, SUM(A.SALDO)
			FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A
			WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
		 					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] B
							WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION)
		END
	END


	INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CATEGORIA]
           ([INDNUEVA]
           ,[IDENTIDAD]
           ,[PERIODO]
           ,[A1]
           ,[A2]
           ,[B1]
           ,[B2]
           ,[C1]
           ,[C2]
           ,[D]
           ,[E]
		   ,SALDO)
     VALUES
           (1
           ,@IDENTIDAD
           ,@PERIODO
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'A1'), 0)
		   ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'A2'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'B1'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'B2'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'C1'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'C2'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'D'), 0)
           ,ISNULL((SELECT CUOTA FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'E'), 0)
  	        ,ISNULL((SELECT SUM(SALDO) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE CATEGORIA = 'X'), 0))
END

GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Indicadores_Cartera]    Script Date: 23/08/2020 12:32:17 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Indicadores_Cartera]
	@IDENTIDAD NVARCHAR(5),
	@PERIODO DATE
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	DECLARE @CARTERA_TOTAL DECIMAL(32,5)
	DECLARE @CANTIDADOPERACIONES INT
	DECLARE @CANTIDADDEUDORES INT
	DECLARE @HH100 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)		
	DECLARE @HH100D AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
			
	DECLARE @REPESENTATIVIDAD10 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)		
	DECLARE @V009_V015 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)
	DECLARE @V010_V015 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)
	DECLARE @V011_V015 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @V13900000 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @V30000000 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @RESULTADO1 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @RESULTADO2 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @RESULTADO3 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @RESULTADO4 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @RESULTADO5 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @RESULTADO6 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @RESULTADO7 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @RESULTADO8 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @RESULTADO9 AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @CIEN_DEUDORES AS DECIMAL(32, 5)
	DECLARE @CIEN_CREDITOS AS DECIMAL(32, 5)
	DECLARE @VEINTE_CREDITOS AS DECIMAL(32, 5)
	DECLARE @NUM_6MESES AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @NUM_12MESES AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @TOT_NUM_6MESES AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @TOT_NUM_12MESES AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	

	DECLARE @VAL_6MESES AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @VAL_12MESES AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @TOT_VAL_6MESES AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	
	DECLARE @TOT_VAL_12MESES AS DECIMAL(32, 5)	

	DECLARE @EXISTE AS  INT	
	DECLARE @HISTORICO AS  INT	

	SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.Salida_Indicadores_Cartera WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO  

	IF @EXISTE = 0 BEGIN
		
		SELECT @CANTIDADDEUDORES = count(1)
								 FROM(SELECT COUNT(1) CANTIDAD
									  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
									  WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
									  GROUP BY A.DEUDOR
									  UNION ALL
									  SELECT COUNT(1) CANTIDAD
									  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
									  WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
									    GROUP BY A.DEUDOR) z

		SELECT @CARTERA_TOTAL =  SUM(SALDO), @CANTIDADOPERACIONES = SUM(CANTIDAD)
								 FROM(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDO),0) SALDO, COUNT(1) CANTIDAD
									  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
									  WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
									  UNION ALL
									  SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDO),0) SALDO, COUNT(1) CANTIDAD
									  FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
									  WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD) z

		SELECT @CIEN_DEUDORES =  SUM(SALDO) 
								   FROM(SELECT TOP(100) SUM(SALDO) SALDO
									    FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										GROUP BY DEUDOR
										ORDER BY 1 DESC
										UNION ALL
										SELECT TOP(100) SUM(SALDO) SALDO
										FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										GROUP BY DEUDOR
										ORDER BY 1 DESC) z

	   SELECT @CIEN_CREDITOS =  SUM(SALDO) 
								   FROM(SELECT TOP(100) SALDO
									    FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										ORDER BY SALDO DESC
										UNION ALL
										SELECT TOP(100) SALDO
										FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										ORDER BY SALDO DESC) z

		SELECT @VEINTE_CREDITOS =  SUM(SALDO) 
								   FROM(SELECT TOP(20) SALDO
									    FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										ORDER BY SALDO DESC
										UNION ALL
										SELECT TOP(20) SALDO
										FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										ORDER BY SALDO DESC) z

		SELECT @HH100 =  ISNULL(SUM(((A.SALDO/@CIEN_CREDITOS)*100)*(((A.SALDO/@CIEN_CREDITOS))*100)),0) 
								   FROM(SELECT TOP(100) SALDO
									    FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										ORDER BY SALDO DESC
										UNION ALL
										SELECT TOP(100) SALDO
										FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										ORDER BY SALDO DESC) A
	
		SELECT @HH100D =  ISNULL(SUM(((A.SALDO/@CIEN_DEUDORES)*100)*(((A.SALDO/@CIEN_DEUDORES))*100)),0) 
								   FROM(SELECT TOP(100) SUM(SALDO) SALDO
									    FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										GROUP BY DEUDOR
										ORDER BY 1 DESC
										UNION ALL
										SELECT TOP(100) SUM(SALDO) SALDO
										FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										GROUP BY DEUDOR
										ORDER BY 1 DESC) A

		SELECT @REPESENTATIVIDAD10 =  ISNULL(SUM(((A.SALDO/@CARTERA_TOTAL)*100)),0) 
									 FROM(SELECT TOP(@CANTIDADDEUDORES/10)  SUM(SALDO) SALDO
									    FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										GROUP BY A.DEUDOR
										ORDER BY SALDO DESC
										UNION ALL
										SELECT TOP(@CANTIDADDEUDORES/10) SUM(SALDO) SALDO
										FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
										WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
										GROUP BY A.DEUDOR
										ORDER BY SALDO DESC) A
		

		SELECT @V009_V015 =   SUM(SALDO) 
							  FROM(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDO),0) saldo
								 FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
								 WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
								 AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA IN ('V009','V010','V011','V012','V013','V014','V015')
								 UNION ALL
								 SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDO),0) saldo
								 FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
								 WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
								 AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA IN ('V009','V010','V011','V012','V013','V014','V015')) z

		SELECT @V010_V015 =   SUM(SALDO) 
							  FROM(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDO),0) saldo
								 FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
								 WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
								 AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA IN ('V010','V011','V012','V013','V014','V015')
								 UNION ALL
								 SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDO),0) saldo
								 FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
								 WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
								 AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA IN ('V010','V011','V012','V013','V014','V015')) z

		SELECT @V011_V015 =  SUM(SALDO) 
							  FROM(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDO),0) saldo
								 FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A WITH(NOLOCK)
								 WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
								 AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA IN ('V011','V012','V013','V014','V015')
								 UNION ALL
								 SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDO),0) saldo
								 FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A WITH(NOLOCK)
								 WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
								 AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA IN ('V011','V012','V013','V014','V015')) z
								 		

		SELECT @V13900000 = SUM(SALDOFINAL)
							FROM(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDOFINAL),0) SALDOFINAL
								FROM SALIDA_BALANCE_COMPROBACION A WITH(NOLOCK)
								WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO 
								AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
								AND A.CUENTA = '13900000') Z

								
		SELECT @V30000000 = SUM(SALDOFINAL)
							FROM(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDOFINAL),0) SALDOFINAL
								FROM SALIDA_BALANCE_COMPROBACION A WITH(NOLOCK)
								WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO 
								AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD
								AND A.CUENTA = '30000000') Z

		BEGIN TRY
			SELECT @RESULTADO1 =  isnull((@VEINTE_CREDITOS / @V30000000) * 100, 0)
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SELECT @RESULTADO1 = 0
		END CATCH

		BEGIN TRY
			SELECT @RESULTADO2 =  isnull((@V010_V015/@V009_V015) * 100, 0)
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SELECT @RESULTADO2 = 0
		END CATCH

		BEGIN TRY
			SELECT @RESULTADO3 = isnull(@V011_V015 /@V009_V015, 0) * 100
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SELECT @RESULTADO3 = 0
		END CATCH

		BEGIN TRY
			SELECT @RESULTADO4 = isnull((@V13900000 / @V009_V015), 0) * 100
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SELECT @RESULTADO4 = 0
		END CATCH

		BEGIN TRY
			SELECT @RESULTADO5 = isnull((@V13900000 / @V010_V015), 0)  * 100
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SELECT @RESULTADO5 = 0
		END CATCH

		SELECT @HISTORICO = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO  

		IF @HISTORICO > 0 BEGIN

			SELECT @TOT_NUM_6MESES = COUNT(1), @TOT_VAL_6MESES = SUM(A.SALDO)
								FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO A
 								WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO --AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA != 'V009'
								AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
											FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B
											WHERE B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
											AND B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -6, @PERIODO)
											AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
															FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS C
															WHERE C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION AND C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -7, @PERIODO)))

			SELECT @NUM_6MESES = COUNT(1), @VAL_6MESES = SUM(A.SALDO)
								FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO A
 								WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA != 'V009'
								AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
											FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B
											WHERE B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
											AND B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -6, @PERIODO)
											AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
															FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS C
															WHERE C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION AND C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -7, @PERIODO)))


			SELECT @TOT_NUM_12MESES = COUNT(1), @TOT_VAL_12MESES = SUM(A.SALDO)
								FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO A
 								WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO --AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA != 'V009'
								AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
											FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B
											WHERE B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
											AND B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -12, @PERIODO)
											AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
															FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS C
															WHERE C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION AND C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -13, @PERIODO)))

			SELECT @NUM_12MESES = COUNT(1), @VAL_12MESES = SUM(A.SALDO)
								FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO A
 								WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA != 'V009'
								AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
											FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B
											WHERE B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
											AND B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -12, @PERIODO)
											AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
															FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS C
															WHERE C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION AND C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -13, @PERIODO)))
		END ELSE BEGIN

			SELECT @TOT_NUM_6MESES = COUNT(1), @TOT_VAL_6MESES = SUM(A.SALDO)
								FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS A
 								WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO --AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA != 'V009'
								AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
											FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B
											WHERE B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
											AND B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -6, @PERIODO)
											AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
															FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS C
															WHERE C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION AND C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -7, @PERIODO)))

			SELECT @NUM_6MESES = COUNT(1), @VAL_6MESES = SUM(A.SALDO)
								FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS A
 								WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA != 'V009'
								AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
											FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B
											WHERE B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
											AND B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -6, @PERIODO)
											AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
															FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS C
															WHERE C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION AND C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -7, @PERIODO)))

			
			SELECT @TOT_NUM_12MESES = COUNT(1), @TOT_VAL_12MESES = SUM(A.SALDO)
								FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS A
 								WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO --AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA != 'V009'
								AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
											FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B
											WHERE B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
											AND B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -12, @PERIODO)
											AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
															FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS C
															WHERE C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION AND C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -13, @PERIODO)))
			
			SELECT @NUM_12MESES = COUNT(1), @VAL_12MESES = SUM(A.SALDO)
								FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS A
 								WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.DIASATRASOALERTA != 'V009'
								AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
											FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B
											WHERE B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD
											AND B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -12, @PERIODO)
											AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 
															FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS C
															WHERE C.IDENTIDAD = B.IDENTIDAD AND C.OPERACION = B.OPERACION AND C.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -13, @PERIODO)))

		END

		BEGIN TRY
			SELECT @RESULTADO6 = isnull((@NUM_6MESES / @TOT_NUM_6MESES) * 100, 0)
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SELECT @RESULTADO6 = 0
		END CATCH

		BEGIN TRY
			SELECT @RESULTADO7 = isnull((@NUM_12MESES / @TOT_NUM_12MESES) * 100, 0)
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SELECT @RESULTADO7 = 0
		END CATCH

		BEGIN TRY
			SELECT @RESULTADO8 = isnull((@VAL_6MESES / @TOT_VAL_6MESES) * 100, 0)
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SELECT @RESULTADO8 = 0
		END CATCH

		BEGIN TRY
			SELECT @RESULTADO9 = isnull((@VAL_12MESES / @TOT_VAL_12MESES) * 100, 0)
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SELECT @RESULTADO9 = 0
		END CATCH
		
		INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_INDICADORES_CARTERA]
			   (IDINDICADOR
			   ,[PERIODO]
			   ,[IDENTIDAD]
			   ,[PORCENTAJE])
			SELECT 1, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @HH100 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 2, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @HH100D AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 3, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD,	@REPESENTATIVIDAD10 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 4, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @RESULTADO1 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 5, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @RESULTADO2 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 6, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @RESULTADO3 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 7, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @RESULTADO4 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 8, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD ,@RESULTADO5 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 9, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @NUM_6MESES AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 10, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @RESULTADO6 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 11, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @NUM_12MESES AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 12, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @RESULTADO7 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 13, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @VAL_6MESES AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 14, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @RESULTADO8 AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 15, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @VAL_12MESES AS PORCENTAJE
			UNION
			SELECT 16, @PERIODO, @IDENTIDAD, @RESULTADO9 AS PORCENTAJE
			
	END
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Maduracion_Cartera]    Script Date: 23/08/2020 12:32:17 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Maduracion_Cartera]
	@IDENTIDAD NVARCHAR(5),
	@PERIODO DATE
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	DECLARE @CANTIDAD AS INT
	DECLARE @EXISTE AS  INT
	DECLARE @ULTIMODIA AS DATE

    SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO

	IF @EXISTE = 0 BEGIN

		SELECT @CANTIDAD = COUNT(1)
		FROM FGA.DBO.XML_ENCABEZADO A WITH(NOLOCK), FGA.DBO.ARCHIVOESTADO B, FGA.DBO.TIPOXML C
		WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD_ID = @IDENTIDAD
		AND A.IDESTADO_ID = B.ID AND B.ID = 3
		AND C.ID = A.IDARCHIVO_ID AND C.ID IN ('302','304','201', '303')
				
		SELECT @ULTIMODIA =  DATEADD(DD,-(DAY(DATEADD(MM,1, @PERIODO))),DATEADD(MM,1,@PERIODO))

		IF(@CANTIDAD = 4) BEGIN
			
			INSERT INTO FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA
			 ([IDENTIDAD]
			,[PERIODO]
			,[IDOPERACION]
			,[CATEGORIARIESGO]
			,[TIPOCARTERA]
			,[SALDO]
			,[TASAINTERES]
			,[PLAZORESTANTE]
			,[CUOTACREDITO])
			SELECT ENTIDAD, PERIODO, IDOPERACION, CATEGORIA, TIPOCARTERA, SALDOPRINCIPAL, TASAINTERESNOMINALVIGENTE, PLAZORESTANTE, CUOTACREDITO
			FROM (SELECT @IDENTIDAD AS ENTIDAD, @PERIODO AS PERIODO, A.IDOPERACION,
				(SELECT C.CATEGORIARIESGO
						FROM XML_CREDITO_DEUDOR C WITH(NOLOCK), XML_ENCABEZADO D WITH(NOLOCK)
						WHERE C.IDDEUDOR = A.IDDEUDOR
							AND D.ID = C.IDENCABEZADO_ID
							AND D.PERIODO = @PERIODO
							AND D.IDENTIDAD_ID = B.IDENTIDAD_ID
							AND D.IDESTADO_ID = 3
							AND D.IDARCHIVO_ID = '302') AS CATEGORIA, 
						A.TIPOCARTERA,
						A.SALDOPRINCIPAL,
						A.TASAINTERESNOMINALVIGENTE,
						DATEDIFF(MONTH, @ULTIMODIA, A.FECHAVENCIMIENTO) AS PLAZORESTANTE,
						CAST(ROUND((A.TASAINTERESNOMINALVIGENTE/12/100) * 
						(POWER((1+(A.TASAINTERESNOMINALVIGENTE/12/100)), DATEDIFF(MONTH, @ULTIMODIA, A.FECHAVENCIMIENTO)))						
						* A.SALDOPRINCIPAL/
						((POWER((1+(A.TASAINTERESNOMINALVIGENTE/12/100)), DATEDIFF(MONTH, @ULTIMODIA, A.FECHAVENCIMIENTO))) - 1), 2) AS DECIMAL(18, 2)) AS CUOTACREDITO
			FROM XML_CREDITO_OPERACION_DIRIND A WITH(NOLOCK), XML_ENCABEZADO B WITH(NOLOCK)
			WHERE A.IDENCABEZADO_ID = B.ID
					AND B.PERIODO = @PERIODO 
					AND B.IDENTIDAD_ID = @IDENTIDAD
					AND B.IDESTADO_ID = 3
					AND B.IDARCHIVO_ID = '303'
					AND ((POWER((1+(A.TASAINTERESNOMINALVIGENTE/12/100)), DATEDIFF(MONTH, @ULTIMODIA, A.FECHAVENCIMIENTO))) - 1) > 0
					AND A.FECHAVENCIMIENTO > @ULTIMODIA) Z
			WHERE Z.CATEGORIA IS NOT NULL

			INSERT INTO SALIDA_CALIF_CARTERA (IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, CATEGORIARIESGO, TIPOCARTERA, MONTO) 
			SELECT IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, TRIM(CATEGORIARIESGO), TIPOCARTERA, SUM(SALDO)
			FROM SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA WITH(NOLOCK)
			WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO --AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('A1','A2','B1','B2','C1','C2','D', 'E')
			GROUP BY IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, TRIM(CATEGORIARIESGO), TIPOCARTERA		
			HAVING SUM(SALDO) > 0	

		END
	END
END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Maduracion_Cartera_Det]
	@IDENTIDAD NVARCHAR(5),
	@PERIODO DATE
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	DECLARE @FECHAACTUAL AS DATE
	DECLARE @EXISTE AS BIGINT	
	DECLARE @PLAZOMAYOR AS INT = 0
	DECLARE @DESDE AS INT = 1
	
    SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA_DET WITH(NOLOCK) WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO

	IF @EXISTE = 0 BEGIN
				
		SELECT @PLAZOMAYOR = MAX(PLAZORESTANTE)
			FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA
			WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO;  
  
			--REINICIAR FECHA ACTUAL
			SELECT @FECHAACTUAL = DATEADD(MONTH, 1, @PERIODO)
			CREATE TABLE #RESUMEN(CATEGORIARIESGO NVARCHAR(5), TIPOCARTERA INT , TOTAL DECIMAL (18,2) )
						
			--CICLO				
			WHILE  @PLAZOMAYOR > @DESDE
			BEGIN
				
				DELETE #RESUMEN

				INSERT INTO #RESUMEN (TIPOCARTERA, CATEGORIARIESGO, TOTAL)
				SELECT  TIPOCARTERA, A.CATEGORIARIESGO, SUM(ISNULL(CUOTACREDITO, 0))
				FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA A WITH(NOLOCK)
				WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO 
				AND PLAZORESTANTE BETWEEN @DESDE AND @PLAZOMAYOR
				GROUP BY A.TIPOCARTERA, CATEGORIARIESGO				

				INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_MADURACION_CARTERA_DET]
			   ([IDENTIDAD]
			   ,[PERIODO]
			   ,[FECHA]
			   ,[TOTALCUOTAS]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA0A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA0B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA0C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA0D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA0E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA1A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA1B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA1C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA1D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA1E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA2A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA2B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA2C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA2D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA2E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA3A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA3B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA3C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA3D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA3E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA4A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA4B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA4C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA4D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA4E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA5A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA5B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA5C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA5D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA5E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA6A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA6B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA6C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA6D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA6E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA7A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA7B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA7C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA7D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA7E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA8A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA8B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA8C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA8D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA8E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA9A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA9B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA9C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA9D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA9E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA10A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA10B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA10C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA10D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA10E]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA11A]
   			   ,[TIPOCARTERA11B]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA11C]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA11D]
			   ,[TIPOCARTERA11E])
				SELECT @IDENTIDAD, @PERIODO, @FECHAACTUAL, 
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(Total) FROM #resumen), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '0' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('A1','A2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '0' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('B1','B2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '0' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('C1','C2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT TOTAL FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '0' AND CATEGORIARIESGO = 'D'), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT TOTAL FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '0' AND CATEGORIARIESGO = 'E'), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '1' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('A1','A2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '1' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('B1','B2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '1' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('C1','C2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT TOTAL FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '1' AND CATEGORIARIESGO = 'D'), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT TOTAL FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '1' AND CATEGORIARIESGO = 'E'), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '2' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('A1','A2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '2' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('B1','B2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '2' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('C1','C2')), 0),

				ISNULL((SELECT TOTAL FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '2' AND CATEGORIARIESGO = 'E'), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '3' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('A1','A2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '3' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('B1','B2')), 0),
				ISNULL((SELECT SUM(TOTAL) FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '3' AND CATEGORIARIESGO IN ('C1','C2')), 0),

				ISNULL((SELECT TOTAL FROM #RESUMEN WHERE TIPOCARTERA = '4' AND CATEGORIARIESGO = 'D'), 0),

	END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Matrices]    Script Date: 23/08/2020 12:32:17 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Matrices]
	@IDENTIDAD NVARCHAR(5),
	@PERIODO DATE
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	DECLARE @EXISTE AS  INT	
	DECLARE @CAMEL AS  INT	
	DECLARE @PERIODOVIGENTE AS  INT	

    SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MATRICES WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO
	SELECT @CAMEL = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_CAMEL WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO

	/*MATRICES*/
	IF @EXISTE = 0 AND @CAMEL > 0 BEGIN
		
		SELECT @PERIODOVIGENTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO
		
		IF @PERIODOVIGENTE > 0 BEGIN

			INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_MATRICES]
			   ([IDENTIDAD]
			   ,[PERIODO]
			   ,[FILA_INICIO]
			   ,[ID_INICIO]
			   ,[COLUMNA_FINAL]
			   ,[ID_FINAL]
			   ,[SALDO]
			   ,[CANTIDAD])
				SELECT IDENTIDAD, @PERIODO,  
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOINICIAL), ATRASOINICIAL,
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOFINAL), ATRASOFINAL, 
				SUM(SALDO), COUNT(1) 
				FROM (SELECT IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, (SELECT MAX(D.ID) FROM DIASATRASO D WHERE A.DIASATRASOMATRIS = D.NOMBRE) ATRASOFINAL, 
																(SELECT MAX(C.ID)
																FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_his B, DIASATRASO C
																WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -12, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
																AND B.DIASATRASOMATRIS = C.NOMBRE) ATRASOINICIAL, SALDO
					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A
					WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO) Z
				WHERE ATRASOINICIAL IS NOT NULL
				GROUP BY IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, ATRASOINICIAL, ATRASOFINAL
				ORDER BY ATRASOINICIAL ASC, ATRASOFINAL ASC
		
		END ELSE BEGIN

			INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_MATRICES]
			   ([IDENTIDAD]
			   ,[PERIODO]
			   ,[FILA_INICIO]
			   ,[ID_INICIO]
			   ,[COLUMNA_FINAL]
			   ,[ID_FINAL]
			   ,[SALDO]
			   ,[CANTIDAD])
				SELECT IDENTIDAD, @PERIODO,  
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOINICIAL), ATRASOINICIAL,
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOFINAL), ATRASOFINAL, 
				SUM(SALDO), COUNT(1) 
				FROM (SELECT IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, (SELECT MAX(D.ID) FROM DIASATRASO D WHERE A.DIASATRASOMATRIS = D.NOMBRE) ATRASOFINAL, 
																(SELECT MAX(C.ID)
																FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B, DIASATRASO C
																WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -12, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
																AND B.DIASATRASOMATRIS = C.NOMBRE) ATRASOINICIAL, SALDO
					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A
					WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO) Z
				WHERE ATRASOINICIAL IS NOT NULL
				GROUP BY IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, ATRASOINICIAL, ATRASOFINAL
				ORDER BY ATRASOINICIAL ASC, ATRASOFINAL ASC
		END	

		/*PERDIDA CALCULADA*/
		EXEC FGA_GENERAR_MATRICES_PERDIDA_ESTIMADA  @IDENTIDAD, @PERIODO

		/*MATRIZ MENSUAL*/
		EXEC FGA_GENERAR_MATRICES_MENSUAL  @IDENTIDAD, @PERIODO

	END
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Matrices_Mensual]    Script Date: 23/08/2020 12:32:17 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Matrices_Mensual]
	@IDENTIDAD NVARCHAR(5),
	@PERIODO DATE
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	DECLARE @EXISTE AS  INT	
	DECLARE @CAMEL AS  INT	
	DECLARE @PERIODOVIGENTE AS  INT	

    SELECT @EXISTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MATRICES_MENSUAL WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO
	
	/*MATRICES*/
	IF @EXISTE = 0 BEGIN
		
		SELECT @PERIODOVIGENTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO =  DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO)
		
		IF @PERIODOVIGENTE > 0 BEGIN

			INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_MATRICES_MENSUAL]
			   ([IDENTIDAD]
			   ,[PERIODO]
			   ,[FILA_INICIO]
			   ,[ID_INICIO]
			   ,[COLUMNA_FINAL]
			   ,[ID_FINAL]
			   ,[SALDO]
			   ,[CANTIDAD])
				SELECT IDENTIDAD, @PERIODO,  
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOINICIAL), ATRASOINICIAL,
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOFINAL), ATRASOFINAL, 
				SUM(SALDO), COUNT(1) 
				FROM (SELECT IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, (SELECT MAX(D.ID) FROM DIASATRASO D WHERE A.DIASATRASOMATRIS = D.NOMBRE) ATRASOFINAL, 
																(SELECT MAX(C.ID)
																FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO B, DIASATRASO C
																WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
																AND B.DIASATRASOMATRIS = C.NOMBRE) ATRASOINICIAL, SALDO
					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A
					WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO) Z
				WHERE ATRASOINICIAL IS NOT NULL
				GROUP BY IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, ATRASOINICIAL, ATRASOFINAL
				ORDER BY ATRASOINICIAL ASC, ATRASOFINAL ASC
		
		END ELSE BEGIN

 			SELECT @PERIODOVIGENTE = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO =  @PERIODO
  		    
			IF @PERIODOVIGENTE > 0 BEGIN
	
				INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_MATRICES_MENSUAL]
			   ([IDENTIDAD]
			   ,[PERIODO]
			   ,[FILA_INICIO]
			   ,[ID_INICIO]
			   ,[COLUMNA_FINAL]
			   ,[ID_FINAL]
			   ,[SALDO]
			   ,[CANTIDAD])
				SELECT IDENTIDAD, @PERIODO,  
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOINICIAL), ATRASOINICIAL,
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOFINAL), ATRASOFINAL, 
				SUM(SALDO), COUNT(1) 
				FROM (SELECT IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, (SELECT MAX(D.ID) FROM DIASATRASO D WHERE A.DIASATRASOMATRIS = D.NOMBRE) ATRASOFINAL, 
																(SELECT MAX(C.ID)
																FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B, DIASATRASO C
																WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
																AND B.DIASATRASOMATRIS = C.NOMBRE) ATRASOINICIAL, SALDO
					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO] A
					WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO) Z
				WHERE ATRASOINICIAL IS NOT NULL
				GROUP BY IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, ATRASOINICIAL, ATRASOFINAL
				ORDER BY ATRASOINICIAL ASC, ATRASOFINAL ASC
			
			END ELSE BEGIN
		
				INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_MATRICES_MENSUAL]
			   ([IDENTIDAD]
			   ,[PERIODO]
			   ,[FILA_INICIO]
			   ,[ID_INICIO]
			   ,[COLUMNA_FINAL]
			   ,[ID_FINAL]
			   ,[SALDO]
			   ,[CANTIDAD])
				SELECT IDENTIDAD, @PERIODO,  
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOINICIAL), ATRASOINICIAL,
				(SELECT NOMBRE FROM DIASATRASO WHERE ID = ATRASOFINAL), ATRASOFINAL, 
				SUM(SALDO), COUNT(1) 
				FROM (SELECT IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, (SELECT MAX(D.ID) FROM DIASATRASO D WHERE A.DIASATRASOMATRIS = D.NOMBRE) ATRASOFINAL, 
																(SELECT MAX(C.ID)
																FROM SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS B, DIASATRASO C
																WHERE B.PERIODO = DATEADD(MONTH, -1, @PERIODO) AND B.IDENTIDAD = A.IDENTIDAD AND B.OPERACION = A.OPERACION
																AND B.DIASATRASOMATRIS = C.NOMBRE) ATRASOINICIAL, SALDO
					FROM [FGA].[DBO].[SALIDA_CARTERA_CREDITO_HIS] A
					WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO) Z
				WHERE ATRASOINICIAL IS NOT NULL
				GROUP BY IDENTIDAD, PERIODO, ATRASOINICIAL, ATRASOFINAL
				ORDER BY ATRASOINICIAL ASC, ATRASOFINAL ASC
			
			END	
		END	
	END
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Matrices_Perdida_Estimada]    Script Date: 23/08/2020 12:32:17 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[FGA_Generar_Matrices_Perdida_Estimada]
	@IDENTIDAD NVARCHAR(5),
	@PERIODO DATE
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	
	DECLARE @CANTIDAD AS  INT	
	DECLARE @TOTAL AS DECIMAL (32,5)	
	DECLARE @C13000000 AS DECIMAL (32, 5)
	DECLARE @C13900000 AS DECIMAL (32, 5)
	DECLARE @PERDIDA_ESTIMADA AS DECIMAL (32, 5)

	SELECT @CANTIDAD = ISNULL(COUNT(1), 0) FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MATRICES WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD GROUP BY PERIODO

	IF @CANTIDAD > 0 BEGIN

		SELECT @C13000000 = SUM(SALDOFINAL)
							FROM(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDOFINAL),0) SALDOFINAL
								 FROM SALIDA_BALANCE_COMPROBACION A WITH(NOLOCK)
								 WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND A.CUENTA = '13000000') Z
		
		SELECT @C13900000 = SUM(SALDOFINAL)
							FROM(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A.SALDOFINAL),0) SALDOFINAL
								 FROM SALIDA_BALANCE_COMPROBACION A WITH(NOLOCK)
								 WHERE A.PERIODO = @PERIODO AND A.IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND A.CUENTA = '13900000') Z

		--SET FMTONLY OFF
		CREATE TABLE #SALDO_PROMEDIO(IDENTIDAD NVARCHAR(5), FILA_INICIO NVARCHAR(150), 
							 		 ID_INICIO INT,COLUMNA_FINAL NVARCHAR(150),ID_FINAL INT, 
									 SALDO DECIMAL(32,5), PROMEDIOFILA DECIMAL(32,5), PROMEDIOTOTAL DECIMAL(32,5))

		CREATE TABLE #FILA(ATRASO INT,  SALDO DECIMAL(32,5))
		CREATE TABLE #COLUMNA(ATRASO INT,  SALDO DECIMAL(32,5))

		INSERT INTO #SALDO_PROMEDIO  ([IDENTIDAD]
			   ,[FILA_INICIO]
			   ,[ID_INICIO]
			   ,[COLUMNA_FINAL]
			   ,[ID_FINAL]
			   ,[SALDO])
		SELECT 	[IDENTIDAD]
			   ,[FILA_INICIO]
			   ,[ID_INICIO]
			   ,[COLUMNA_FINAL]
			   ,[ID_FINAL]
			   ,(ISNULL(SUM([SALDO]),0))
		FROM FGA.DBO.SALIDA_MATRICES 
		WHERE IDENTIDAD = @IDENTIDAD AND PERIODO = @PERIODO
		GROUP BY 	[IDENTIDAD], [FILA_INICIO], [ID_INICIO], [COLUMNA_FINAL], [ID_FINAL]

		SELECT @TOTAL = SUM(SALDO) FROM #SALDO_PROMEDIO
	
		UPDATE #SALDO_PROMEDIO
		SET	PROMEDIOTOTAL = (SALDO/@TOTAL) --* 100
	
		SELECT @PERDIDA_ESTIMADA = ISNULL(SUM(PROMEDIOTOTAL),0) FROM #SALDO_PROMEDIO
									WHERE ID_FINAL >= ID_INICIO and 
									ID_FINAL >= 5
	
	    INSERT INTO [DBO].[SALIDA_MATRICES_PERDIDA_ESTIMADA]
           ([IDENTIDAD]
           ,[PERIODO]
           ,[PERDIDA_ESTIMADA]
           ,[ESTIMACION_MATRIZ]
           ,[PORCENTAJE]
		   ,CUENTA139)	
		SELECT @IDENTIDAD, @PERIODO, @PERDIDA_ESTIMADA*100, (@C13000000 * @PERDIDA_ESTIMADA),( @C13000000 * @PERDIDA_ESTIMADA / @C13900000) * 100, @C13900000

	END
END
GO
